public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
            long prod = (long) vec[i] * (long) a;
            if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
            } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
            } else {
                ret[i] = (int) prod;
            }
        }
        return ret;
    }
}

```

    //@ assigns \nothing;

    //@          \fresh(\result) &&
    //@          (\forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length;
    //@              (\longprod(vec[i], a) > Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> \result[i] == Integer.MAX_VALUE) &&
    //@              (\longprod(vec[i], a) < Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> \result[i] == Integer.MIN_VALUE) &&
    //@              (Integer.MIN_VALUE <= \longprod(vec[i], a) && \longprod(vec[i], a) <= Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> \result[i] == (int)\longprod(vec[i], a))
    //@          )
    //@         );
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= vec.length;
        //@ maintaining ret.length == vec.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i;
        //@   ( (long)vec[j] * (long)a > Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> ret[j] == Integer.MAX_VALUE ) &&

                ret[i] = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
            } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
            } else {
                ret[i] = (int) prod;
            }
        }
        return ret;
    }

    //@ public normal_behavior
    //@ ensures \result == (long) x * (long) y;

    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {

public class DivArrayByTwo {
    public static void DivArrayByTwo(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null)
            return;

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; ++i) {
            arr[i] = arr[i] / 2;
        }
    }
}

public class DivArrayByTwo {
    //@  requires arr == null;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  assigns arr[*];
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == \old(arr[i]) / 2;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static void DivArrayByTwo(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null)
            return;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] == \old(arr[j]) / 2;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k; i <= k < arr.length; arr[k] == \old(arr[k]);

        //@ decreases arr.length - i;

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;

        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();

        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {

            i = i + 1;
        }

    }

public class Find {
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {
        if (arr == null)
            return -1;

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)
            if (arr[i] == elem)

        return -1;

public class Find {
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  requires \exists int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures 0 <= \result < arr.length;
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < \result; arr[i] != elem;

public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
            long prod = (long) vec[i] * (long) a;
            if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
            } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
            } else {
                ret[i] = (int) prod;
            }
        }
        return ret;
    }
}

```

public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    //@ requires true;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (vec == null && \result == null) || 
    //@         (vec != null && \result != null && \result.length == vec.length &&
    //@          \fresh(\result) &&
    //@          (\forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length;
    //@              (\longprod(vec[i], a) > Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> \result[i] == Integer.MAX_VALUE) &&
    //@              (\longprod(vec[i], a) < Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> \result[i] == Integer.MIN_VALUE) &&
    //@              (Integer.MIN_VALUE <= \longprod(vec[i], a) && \longprod(vec[i], a) <= Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> \result[i] == (int)\longprod(vec[i], a))
    //@          )
    //@         );
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= vec.length;
        //@ maintaining ret.length == vec.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i;
        //@   ( (long)vec[j] * (long)a > Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> ret[j] == Integer.MAX_VALUE ) &&
        //@   ( (long)vec[j] * (long)a < Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> ret[j] == Integer.MIN_VALUE ) &&

        //@ loop_writes ret[*], i;
        //@ decreases vec.length - i;

            long prod = (long) vec[i] * (long) a;
            if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
            } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
            } else {
                ret[i] = (int) prod;
            }
        }
        return ret;
    }

    //@ public normal_behavior
    //@ ensures \result == (long) x * (long) y;
    //@ pure
    private static /*@ pure @*/ long longprod(int x, int y) {
        return (long) x * (long) y;
    }

    //@          // The returned array contains no other modifications or reorderings
    //@          (\forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length; 
    //@              \result[i] == 
    //@                  ((long)vec[i] * (long)a > Integer.MAX_VALUE ? Integer.MAX_VALUE :
    //@                   ( (long)vec[i] * (long)a < Integer.MIN_VALUE ? Integer.MIN_VALUE :
    //@                     (int)((long)vec[i] * (long)a)
    //@                   )
    //@                  )
    //@          )
    //@         );
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= vec.length;
        //@ maintaining ret.length == vec.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i;
        //@   (((long)vec[j] * (long)a) > Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> ret[j] == Integer.MAX_VALUE) &&
        //@   (((long)vec[j] * (long)a) < Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> ret[j] == Integer.MIN_VALUE) &&
        //@   (Integer.MIN_VALUE <= ((long)vec[j] * (long)a) && ((long)vec[j] * (long)a) <= Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> ret[j] == (int)((long)vec[j] * (long)a));
        //@ loop_writes ret[*], i;
        //@ decreases vec.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
            long prod = (long) vec[i] * (long) a;
            if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
            } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
            } else {
                ret[i] = (int) prod;
            }
        }
        return ret;
    }

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))

```
public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    //@ requires true;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (vec == null && \result == null) || 
    //@         (vec != null && \result != null && \result.length == vec.length &&
    //@          \fresh(\result) &&
    //@          (\forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length;
    //@              ((long)vec[i] * (long)a > Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> \result[i] == Integer.MAX_VALUE) &&
    //@              ((long)vec[i] * (long)a < Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> \result[i] == Integer.MIN_VALUE) &&
    //@              (Integer.MIN_VALUE <= (long)vec[i] * (long)a && (long)vec[i] * (long)a <= Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> \result[i] == (int)((long)vec[i] * (long)a))
    //@          )
    //@         );
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= vec.length;
        //@ maintaining ret.length == vec.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i;
        //@   (((long)vec[j] * (long)a) > Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> ret[j] == Integer.MAX_VALUE) &&
        //@   (((long)vec[j] * (long)a) < Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> ret[j] == Integer.MIN_VALUE) &&
        //@   (Integer.MIN_VALUE <= ((long)vec[j] * (long)a) && ((long)vec[j] * (long)a) <= Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> ret[j] == (int)((long)vec[j] * (long)a));
        //@ loop_writes ret[*], i;
        //@ decreases vec.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
            long prod = (long) vec[i] * (long) a;
            if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
            } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
            } else {
                ret[i] = (int) prod;
            }
        }
        return ret;
    }
}

public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    //@ requires true;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (vec == null && \result == null) || 
    //@         (vec != null && \result != null && \result.length == vec.length &&
    //@          \fresh(\result) &&
    //@          \result != vec &&
    //@          // The returned array length matches input array length
    //@          (\forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length; 0 <= i < \result.length) &&
    //@          // Each element of the result is the saturated product of vec[i] and a
    //@          (\forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length;
    //@              ((long)vec[i] * (long)a > Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> \result[i] == Integer.MAX_VALUE) &&
    //@              ((long)vec[i] * (long)a < Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> \result[i] == Integer.MIN_VALUE) &&
    //@              (Integer.MIN_VALUE <= (long)vec[i] * (long)a && (long)vec[i] * (long)a <= Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> \result[i] == (int)((long)vec[i] * (long)a))
    //@          ) &&
    //@          // The returned array contains exactly the saturated products of input elements, no reorderings or omissions
    //@          (\forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length;
    //@              \result[i] == 
    //@                  ((long)vec[i] * (long)a > Integer.MAX_VALUE ? Integer.MAX_VALUE :
    //@                   ((long)vec[i] * (long)a < Integer.MIN_VALUE ? Integer.MIN_VALUE :
    //@                    (int)((long)vec[i] * (long)a)
    //@                   )
    //@                  )
    //@          ) &&
    //@          // No aliasing between input and output arrays
    //@          \result != vec
    //@         );
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= vec.length;
        //@ maintaining ret.length == vec.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i;
        //@   (((long)vec[j] * (long)a) > Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> ret[j] == Integer.MAX_VALUE) &&
        //@   (((long)vec[j] * (long)a) < Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> ret[j] == Integer.MIN_VALUE) &&
        //@   (Integer.MIN_VALUE <= ((long)vec[j] * (long)a) && ((long)vec[j] * (long)a) <= Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> ret[j] == (int)((long)vec[j] * (long)a));
        //@ loop_writes ret[*], i;
        //@ decreases vec.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
            long prod = (long) vec[i] * (long) a;
            if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
            } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
            } else {
                ret[i] = (int) prod;
            }
        }
        return ret;

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    //@ requires true;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (vec == null && \result == null) || 
    //@         (vec != null && \result != null && \result.length == vec.length &&
    //@          \fresh(\result) &&
    //@          \result != vec &&
    //@          (\forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length;
    //@              ((long)vec[i] * (long)a > Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> \result[i] == Integer.MAX_VALUE) &&
    //@              ((long)vec[i] * (long)a < Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> \result[i] == Integer.MIN_VALUE) &&
    //@              (Integer.MIN_VALUE <= (long)vec[i] * (long)a && (long)vec[i] * (long)a <= Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> \result[i] == (int)((long)vec[i] * (long)a))
    //@          ) &&
    //@          // The returned array contains no other modifications or reorderings
    //@          (\forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length; 
    //@              \result[i] == 
    //@                  ((long)vec[i] * (long)a > Integer.MAX_VALUE ? Integer.MAX_VALUE :
    //@                   ( (long)vec[i] * (long)a < Integer.MIN_VALUE ? Integer.MIN_VALUE :
    //@                     (int)((long)vec[i] * (long)a)
    //@                   )
    //@                  )
    //@          )
    //@         );
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= vec.length;
        //@ maintaining ret.length == vec.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i;
        //@   (((long)vec[j] * (long)a) > Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> ret[j] == Integer.MAX_VALUE) &&
        //@   (((long)vec[j] * (long)a) < Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> ret[j] == Integer.MIN_VALUE) &&
        //@   (Integer.MIN_VALUE <= ((long)vec[j] * (long)a) && ((long)vec[j] * (long)a) <= Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> ret[j] == (int)((long)vec[j] * (long)a));
        //@ loop_writes ret[*], i;
        //@ decreases vec.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
            long prod = (long) vec[i] * (long) a;
            if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
            } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
            } else {
                ret[i] = (int) prod;
            }
        }
        return ret;
    }
}

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

    //@          // The returned array contains no other modifications or reorderings
    //@          (\forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length; 
    //@              \result[i] == 
    //@                  ((long)vec[i] * (long)a > Integer.MAX_VALUE ? Integer.MAX_VALUE :
    //@                   ( (long)vec[i] * (long)a < Integer.MIN_VALUE ? Integer.MIN_VALUE :
    //@                     (int)((long)vec[i] * (long)a)
    //@                   )
    //@                  )
    //@          )
    //@         );
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= vec.length;
        //@ maintaining ret.length == vec.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i;
        //@   (((long)vec[j] * (long)a) > Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> ret[j] == Integer.MAX_VALUE) &&
        //@   (((long)vec[j] * (long)a) < Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> ret[j] == Integer.MIN_VALUE) &&
        //@   (Integer.MIN_VALUE <= ((long)vec[j] * (long)a) && ((long)vec[j] * (long)a) <= Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> ret[j] == (int)((long)vec[j] * (long)a));
        //@ loop_writes ret[*], i;
        //@ decreases vec.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
            long prod = (long) vec[i] * (long) a;
            if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
            } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
            } else {
                ret[i] = (int) prod;
            }
        }
        return ret;
    }

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

        //@   (((long)vec[j] * (long)a) < Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> ret[j] == Integer.MIN_VALUE) &&

        //@ loop_writes ret[*], i;
        //@ decreases vec.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {

            if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
            } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {

- Bounds and Ranges: Specify strict limits on indices, sizes, and values.
- State Effects/Frame Conditions: Specify which memory locations may be assigned and exactly how.
- Minimality/Maximality: Specify conditions related to the search (e.g., no earlier index satisfies the predicate) or "not found" conditions.
- Ordering and Permutation: If the code sorts or reorders, specify preservation of elements (permutation) and the sorting order.
- Allocation/Aliasing: Use "\fresh(\result)" and specify non-aliasing relationships when the code returns new allocations.

public class ScalarProductSaturated {
    //@ requires true;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (vec == null && \result == null) || 
    //@         (vec != null && \result != null && \result.length == vec.length &&
    //@          \fresh(\result) &&
    //@          \result != vec &&
    //@          // The returned array length matches input array length
    //@          (\forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length; 0 <= i < \result.length) &&
    //@          // Each element of the result is the saturated product of vec[i] and a
    //@          (\forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length;
    //@              ((long)vec[i] * (long)a > Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> \result[i] == Integer.MAX_VALUE) &&
    //@              ((long)vec[i] * (long)a < Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> \result[i] == Integer.MIN_VALUE) &&
    //@              (Integer.MIN_VALUE <= (long)vec[i] * (long)a && (long)vec[i] * (long)a <= Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> \result[i] == (int)((long)vec[i] * (long)a))
    //@          ) &&
    //@          // The returned array contains exactly the saturated products of input elements, no reorderings or omissions
    //@          (\forall int i; 0 <= i < vec.length;
    //@              \result[i] == 
    //@                  ((long)vec[i] * (long)a > Integer.MAX_VALUE ? Integer.MAX_VALUE :
    //@                   ((long)vec[i] * (long)a < Integer.MIN_VALUE ? Integer.MIN_VALUE :
    //@                    (int)((long)vec[i] * (long)a)
    //@                   )
    //@                  )
    //@          ) &&
    //@          // No aliasing between input and output arrays
    //@          \result != vec &&
    //@          // No other heap locations are modified
    //@          \assignable \nothing;
    //@         );
    public static int[] scalarProductSaturated(int[] vec, int a) {
        if (vec == null) return null;
        int ret[] = new int[vec.length];
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= vec.length;
        //@ maintaining ret.length == vec.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i;
        //@   (((long)vec[j] * (long)a) > Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> ret[j] == Integer.MAX_VALUE) &&
        //@   (((long)vec[j] * (long)a) < Integer.MIN_VALUE ==> ret[j] == Integer.MIN_VALUE) &&
        //@   (Integer.MIN_VALUE <= ((long)vec[j] * (long)a) && ((long)vec[j] * (long)a) <= Integer.MAX_VALUE ==> ret[j] == (int)((long)vec[j] * (long)a));
        //@ loop_writes ret[*], i;
        //@ decreases vec.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < vec.length; i++) {
            long prod = (long) vec[i] * (long) a;
            if (prod > Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
            } else if (prod < Integer.MIN_VALUE) {
                ret[i] = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
            } else {
                ret[i] = (int) prod;
            }
        }
        return ret;
    }
}
